Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electricity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 275. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 901 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 275 + O(1/n^901). Summing over all modes and applying the electricity convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
